## 🤔 What is GPT Image 2?

**GPT Image 2** (codename **"duct-tape"**) is OpenAI's next-generation image model. Community testing highlights a leap in these areas:

- 🎯 **Pixel-Perfect Text Rendering** — Native-quality text in Chinese, English, and Japanese, no typos or warped glyphs
- 🎨 **Cross-Image Consistency** — The same character, style, or IP stays identical across a series, down to the pixel
- ⚡ **Commercial-Grade Illustration** — Illustration output is ready to ship without manual polish
- 🌈 **True Art Style Induction** — Creatively evokes the feeling of a style rather than merely approximating references
- 🔧 **Storyboard & Product Series** — Ideal for storyboards, IP characters, and multi-panel product visuals
- 📐 **Multi-Language Design** — Social cards, banners, and posters with accurate multilingual typography in one shot

📚 **Learn More:** See community testing in our [report summary](docs/FAQ.md)

### 🚀 Raycast Integration

Some prompts support **dynamic arguments** using [Raycast Snippets](https://raycast.com/help/snippets) syntax. Look for the 🚀 Raycast Friendly badge!

**Example:**
```
A quote card with "{argument name="quote" default="Stay hungry, stay foolish"}"
by {argument name="author" default="Steve Jobs"}
```

When used in Raycast, you can dynamically replace the arguments for quick iterations!

### No. 1: VR Headset Exploded View Poster

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a high-tech exploded view diagram of a VR headset with detailed component callouts and promotional text.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "exploded view product diagram poster",
  "subject": "VR headset",
  "style": "clean high-tech 3D render, studio lighting, glowing accents",
  "background": "{argument name=\"background color\" default=\"soft purple and blue gradient\"}",
  "header": {
    "logo": "∞ {argument name=\"product name\" default=\"Meta Quest 3\"}",
    "subtitle": "{argument name=\"main catchphrase\" default=\"まったく新しい現実を、まったく新しい構造から。\"}"
  },
  "layout": {
    "centerpiece": "vertically stacked exploded view of a VR headset showing 9 distinct layers of internal components: outer shell, camera sensors, motherboard with chip, pancake lenses, internal frame, battery packs, side straps, top strap, and facial interface cushion.",
    "callout_labels": {
      "count": 8,
      "left_side": [
        "Snapdragon® XR2 Gen 2\n圧倒的な処理性能でリアルタイムな体験を。",
        "調整可能なIPD機構\n幅広いユーザーに快適なフィット感を。",
        "精密設計されたヘッドストラップ\n快適さと安定性を追求したエルゴノミクス。"
      ],
      "right_side": [
        "フェイスプレート\n洗練されたデザインと最適な重量バランス。",
        "トラッキングカメラ\n高精度な位置トラッキングと環境認識を実現。",
        "パンケーキレンズ\n薄型設計で広い視野角と鮮明な映像を提供。",
        "高性能バッテリー\n長時間駆動を支える最適化された電源設計。",
        "柔らかなフェイスインターフェース\n長時間でも快適な装着感を実現。"
      ]
    },
    "footer": {
      "left_text_block": {
        "headline": "{argument name=\"bottom headline\" default=\"体験は、構造から進化する。\"}",
        "body": "一つひとつのパーツに、没入体験を支える最先端テクノロジーとこだわりの設計。Meta Quest 3は、未来を感じさせる体験を内部から生み出しています。"
      },
      "right_logo": "∞ Meta"
    }
  }
}
```

### No. 2: Illustrated City Food Map

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a hand-drawn, watercolor-style tourist map featuring numbered local food specialties, landmarks, and a legend.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "illustrated map infographic",
  "style": "{argument name=\"art style\" default=\"watercolor and ink hand-drawn illustration on vintage parchment\"}",
  "title_section": {
    "text": "{argument name=\"city name\" default=\"成都\"} {argument name=\"map title\" default=\"吃货暴走地图\"}",
    "mascot": "cartoon red chili pepper wearing sunglasses and giving a thumbs up"
  },
  "border": "{argument name=\"border decoration\" default=\"vine of green leaves and red chili peppers\"}",
  "layout": {
    "background": "textured beige parchment paper with yellow roads, blue rivers, and green park areas",
    "sections": [
      {
        "title": "landmarks",
        "count": 6,
        "illustrations": ["traditional pavilion", "traditional monastery", "modern skyscraper with climbing panda", "tall TV tower", "traditional gate", "industrial buildings"],
        "labels": ["人民公园", "文殊院", "IFS", "339电视塔", "宽窄巷子", "东郊记忆"]
      },
      {
        "title": "food_spots",
        "count": 12,
        "illustrations": ["mapo tofu", "dumplings in chili oil", "skewers in pot", "sticky rice balls", "egg baking cake", "nine-grid hotpot", "sweet potato noodles", "cold skewers", "spicy mixed dish", "covered tea bowl", "ice jelly dessert", "spicy rabbit heads"],
        "labels": ["1 陈麻婆豆腐", "2 钟水饺", "3 春熙路", "4 宽窄巷子·三大炮", "5 建设路·叶婆婆蛋烘糕", "6 玉林路·小龙坎火锅", "7 香香巷·肥肠粉", "8 武侯祠大街·钵钵鸡", "9 东郊记忆·冒椒火辣", "10 人民公园·鹤鸣茶社", "11 锦里古街·冰粉", "12 双流老妈兔头"]
      },
      {
        "title": "图例",
        "position": "bottom-right",
        "count": 5,
        "items": ["red dot", "green house", "green tree", "blue line", "yellow double line"],
        "labels": ["美食地点", "地标景点", "公园绿地", "河流湖泊", "主要道路"]
      }
    ],
    "centerpiece": "giant panda sitting and eating bamboo",
    "bottom_right_extras": ["vintage compass rose with N, S, E, W", "disclaimer text '温馨提示：吃辣需谨慎，肠胃要保护~' with a red chili pepper icon"]

}
```

### No. 4: E-commerce Live Stream UI Mockup

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a realistic social media live stream interface overlaying a portrait, featuring customizable chat messages, gift popups, and a product purchase card.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "live stream UI mockup",
  "subject": {
    "description": "portrait of {argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}, smiling, wearing a black t-shirt with a white technical schematic graphic",
    "background": "left side shows a screen with '{argument name=\"left background logo\" default=\"SPACEX\"}' text, right side shows a red '{argument name=\"right background logo\" default=\"Tesla T logo\"}' and a dark car"
  },
  "ui_overlay": {
    "top_header": {
      "host_info": "avatar, name '{argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}', subtext '55.6万本场点赞', red '关注' button",
      "rank_badge": "gold coin icon with '全站第1名'",
      "viewer_stats": "3 top viewer avatars with '12.3w', '8.6w', '5.7w', total '68.7万', 'X' close button",
      "right_links": "'更多直播 >', '礼物展馆 0/24' with blue '经典' tag"
    },
    "mid_left_gifts": {
      "count": 2,
      "items": [
        "avatar '科技爱好者', '送小心心', heart icon x 1314",
        "avatar '星辰大海', '送火箭', rocket icon x 666"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_left_chat": {
      "system_message": "level 37 badge '宇宙漫游者 加入了直播间'",
      "message_count": 7,
      "messages": [
        "小火箭: 马斯克！未来可期！🚀",
        "future: 特斯拉Model 2什么时候出？",
        "星空梦想家: SpaceX今年能上火星吗？",
        "AI探索者: Neuralink进展如何？",
        "帅气的网友: 马总好！",
        "Mars: 第一次来你的直播，超激动！",
        "用户123: 讲讲AI吧，会取代人类吗？"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_right_product_card": {
      "hot_tag": "orange '热卖 x 1888'",
      "image": "Tesla Cybertruck",
      "title": "{argument name=\"product name\" default=\"特斯拉Cybertruck 电动皮卡\"}",
      "price": "{argument name=\"product price\" default=\"¥ 1,618,000\"}",
      "button": "red '抢' button",
      "floating_animation": "translucent hearts floating up the right edge"
    },
    "bottom_bar": {
      "input_field": "'说点什么...'",
      "icons": ["smiley face", "three dots", "shopping cart", "gift box", "share"]
    }
  }
}
```

### No. 5: Anime Martial Arts Battle Illustration

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a dynamic, high-impact anime illustration of two female characters fighting in a traditional dojo with elemental energy effects.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
An anime-style illustration of a {argument name="action type" default="high-impact martial arts battle"} between two young female fighters in a {argument name="setting" default="traditional wooden martial arts dojo"}. In the foreground, a girl with black hair in a high bun wears a {argument name="character 1 color theme" default="red and white"} Chinese-style martial arts outfit with baggy pants. She is in a dynamic, low, forward-thrusting stance, surrounded by swirling red energy and water splashes. - Keep the original hairstyle recognizable while translating it into loose sketchy strokes
```

```
POSE & EXPRESSION:
- Preserve the main pose and body direction from the reference
- Give the character a cute, cheerful, playful expression
- Natural hand gestures and expressive body language
- Add small doodle elements around the character that match the mood, such as hearts, stars, crowns, sparkles, motion lines, flowers, or tiny symbols

DRAWING DETAILS:
- Black sketch lines should be slightly uneven, textured, and imperfect
- Use loose cross-hatching and scribbled shading where appropriate
- Pastel pink, yellow, blue, or other soft colors may be used sparingly for cheeks and decorative doodles
- Keep the illustration mostly monochrome with selective colorful accents
- White background with plenty of negative space
- Make it feel like someone personally drew the character with a black marker or pencil on paper

COMPOSITION:
- Center the character prominently
- Keep the framing similar to the reference photo
- Add a few cute surrounding doodles without distracting from the character
- Clean, simple composition suitable for a profile picture, sticker, social media post, or cute illustration

IMPORTANT:
Do not make the character photorealistic. Do not make it overly polished, 3D, glossy, or professionally vectorized.
```

```
Subject: Morning window and white lace relaxation

Main Subject:
A lone woman sitting on a plush white rug in the center of the frame. Dressed in a {argument name="outfit" default="white lace bra top and a light wrap skirt"}, she looks at the camera with one knee raised in a morning room with wood and plants.

Person/Expression:
Her face is tilted slightly to her left, looking straight at the camera with a calm expression and slightly parted lips. She has a rounded oval face, a small chin, almond-shaped eyes with relaxed thin eyebrows, a short slender nose, glossy pale rose lips, and light pink cheeks. Her dark brown shoulder-length bob has sheer bangs and naturally flicked-out ends.

Clothing/Pose:
A white all-over lace triangle bra top and a creamy white thin wrap skirt. The top features thin straps, a deep V-neck, scalloped lace edges, and vertical seams on the cups. She supports herself with her right hand on the rug behind her, raises her right knee high in the center foreground, bends her left leg to the left, and extends her right leg forward.

Background/Light:
To the left are {argument name="windowside items" default="a large window with white curtains and a round wooden table"}, small white flowers, and a milky drink; in the background right are wooden shelves, books, a light, and a plant in a wicker pot. Warm, soft direct light enters from the window on the left, creating bright bands and gentle shadows on the skin, rug, and wooden floor.

Composition/Camera:
3:4 vertical orientation, full-body shot from a horizontal eye-level camera close to the floor. The person occupies most of the center, with the raised knee and extended right leg appearing large in the lower half foreground, with the right foot cut off at the bottom edge. Focus is on the face and upper body, with background shelves softly blurred.

Texture/Style:
A photorealistic indoor photograph with warm natural light. Soft, high-definition rendering of fine lace, thin fabric, plush rug, wood, flowers, and skin.

Negative:
Do not make the raised knee in the foreground smaller; do not omit the vertical seams on the white lace.
```

A long-focus beauty photography prompt for a high-finish Chinese beauty portrait, focusing on 'porcelain skin', bright eyes, and soft pink makeup in a clean studio setting.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A high-completion, photorealistic extreme close-up beauty portrait of a young adult Chinese woman. The subject is approximately 20–26 years old with natural and distinct Chinese female features. The overall temperament is pure, beautiful, clean, soft, agile, and enduringly attractive, with a clear sense of adulthood, not juvenile, not Westernized, not stylized like a generic influencer, and not replicating the specific bone structure of any reference figure, only drawing from ideal skin clarity, eye brightness, delicate pink-toned makeup relationship, close-up lens language, and soft-light beauty photography atmosphere. The character features a 'Type B | Bright-Eyed Cinematic Face.' The face shape and bone structure are a slightly elongated, soft oval, with a full forehead, light cheekbone support, a naturally full mid-face, and a clear yet soft jawline. The overall structure maintains a realistic and reasonable Oriental soft bone structure: naturally full forehead, smooth temples, cheekbones with slight support but not excessive outward expansion, naturally full mid-face, clear and soft jawline, and a delicate but not overly sharp chin, with soft and smooth facial lines, maintaining the aestheticized real-person structure of young Chinese women. The eye design consists of large, bright Oriental almond eyes, with a gradient iris from tea-brown to tea-amber brown, clear aegyo-sal (under-eye bags), and large window-shaped catchlights. The subject has large, bright Oriental almond eyes, horizontally stretched and with a relatively large vertical opening but maintaining real-person proportions, natural and soft double eyelid folds, and clear, soft aegyo-sal; the iris is a transparent tea-brown to tea-amber brown gradient with fine, realistic radial textures, a clear pupil border, and a natural dark brown outer ring. Under large-area frontal soft-light illumination, the light-receiving area of the iris shows subtle levels of honey brown and amber brown. The sclera is bright and clear but not pure white, the cornea is moist and transparent, the inner corners are slightly damp, and the lower eyelid retains an extremely fine, realistic waterline. Both eyes have large window-shaped or rectangular soft-light reflection highlights, making the eyes bright, transparent, moist, soulful, and clear overall, becoming the primary visual center of the portrait. The nose and lips are designed with a delicate, straight bridge, a small and rounded tip, soft cherry lips, and a slightly full lower lip. The root of the nose is natural, the bridge is slender and straight but not excessively sharp, the tip is small and rounded, and the nostrils are contained but retain real structure; the lips are naturally soft with a gentle Cupid's bow, the lower lip is slightly full, retaining slight realistic lip lines, with a soft, moist, and not overly glass-like delicate glow. The skin uses low-frequency, high-clarity cool white porcelain beauty real-person skin. The skin tone is a high-brightness, low-saturation, neutral-to-cool porcelain white base, overall white, pure, and bright, significantly reducing yellow, gray, earthy, and fleshy tones; large areas of the cheeks, forehead, and chin maintain a low-frequency, smooth, quiet, and delicate skin surface, with almost no visible large pores, rough particles, or high-frequency skin noise. Skin realism does not rely on large areas of pores and textures, but is mainly reflected through natural facial structures, subcutaneous light transmission, continuous light and shadow changes, and extremely slight real micro-structures around the nose, eyes, and lips. Large areas of the face remain cool and clean porcelain white, with very low concentrations of pale pink blood color retained only around the eyes, under the eyes, in a small area at the center of the apple muscles, the tip of the nose, and the lips, forming a color relationship where 'white is the main body and pink is the accent.' The forehead, bridge of the nose, apple muscles, and Cupid's bow have wide, soft micro-glows; the skin appears white, delicate, soft, moist, and transparent, but never plastic, never wax-like, and never CG-stylized. The makeup uses low-concentration pink-toned beauty close-up makeup, with pink concentrated around the eyes, under the eyes, and on the lips. The eye area is softly blended with light pink apricot, peach pink, or light tea pink, with a soft pink response between the upper and lower eyelids; the corners of the eyes are slightly extended with an extremely fine natural eyeliner; eyelashes are dark brownish-black or deep brownish-black, slender, well-defined, and naturally curled, with enough presence to form local contrast for the eyes. Blush is only softly concentrated in a small area under the eyes and the center of the apple muscles, not covering the cheeks in a large area. The lips use low-saturation tender pink, bean paste tender pink, or peach pink, presenting a delicate, soft, moist, lightly reflective, and lightly realistic lip line effect. The hairstyle is deep brownish-black smooth long hair, naturally parted in the middle, with a small amount of stray hair on the sides of the ears. The hair strands maintain a real-person photographic texture and a moderate amount of high-frequency detail, with individual strands naturally discernible, without plastic hair strands, wig feel, or hair-piece-style CG edges; the hair overall should not overshadow the subject but serve as a soft foil for the face. Photography uses 100mm extreme close-up frontal beauty portraiture, high-key soft light, and a cool gray-blue background. Using 100mm telephoto beauty portrait lens language to avoid wide-angle perspective distortion. The subject's face occupies about 70%–90% of the frame, with the eyes located in the upper visual center area. Focus is prioritized on the eyes, iris, and eyelashes, with the lips secondary in clarity, and the ears and back hair slightly blurred. The overall image follows the photographic principle of 'low-frequency skin + high-frequency features': large areas of skin have lower local contrast, while key facial features—especially eyelashes, iris, pupils, eyeliner, lip lines, and hair strands—maintain high realism and clarity. Lighting uses a high-key frontal beauty soft light system: a super large soft light source is positioned directly in front of the camera, slightly above the subject's eyes and close to the subject, shining softly downwards, combined with low-intensity soft fill light, making the face overall bright and even, with very shallow and clean shadows in the eye sockets, nose, under the eyes, and under the jaw. Skin highlights are wide and soft, white and transparent but not blown-out white, and shadows remain neutral or slightly cool, not yellowing, not grayish-green, and not dirty. The background uses low-saturation cool gray-blue, with brightness about half to one stop lower than the subject's face, maintaining high simplicity and weakening to highlight the subject's skin tone as whiter, more transparent, and purer. The aspect ratio is 9:16. The overall effect should present: a high-clarity Chinese porcelain-white pure beauty with real-person photographic authenticity, skin that is quiet, white, delicate, and low-frequency, eyes that are bright, moist, transparent, and rich in optical layers, makeup that is light and pink-toned, with the overall look being both realistic and possessing the high-class feel of a high-completion beauty close-up, without producing a plastic, CG, influencer, or uniform high-definition studio feel.
```

```
Main poster: Design an information-dense bilingual atlas poster titled “{argument name="main title" default="城市生命系统图谱"}” with the English subtitle “{argument name="English subtitle" default="Urban Metabolism Atlas"}”. Under the title add the small bilingual tagline “城市是一個有生命的系統 / The City is a Living System”. The poster should look like a professional architectural/urban-planning infographic: off-white background, navy headline text, thin gray panel borders, small bilingual labels, diagrammatic icons, and an isometric cutaway city illustration occupying the central majority.

Poster top elements: On the upper left, include a compass rose marked N and a scale bar labeled “Scale” with tick marks 0, 50, 100, 200, 500m. Near the top center, include a boxed “System Legend” with exactly 10 colored line samples labeled: Electricity, Gas, Heating, Communication, Traffic, Logistics, Data Flow, People Flow, Clean Water, Wastewater. On the upper right, include one small inset map card labeled “Location”, showing a simplified city map with green parks, blue waterways, gray streets, and a red boundary.

Central city illustration: Show a dense isometric smart city with many high-rise towers, mid-rise blocks, roads with cars and buses, a blue canal or river, bridges, street trees, plazas, underground metro tunnels and utility pipes visible in a cutaway at the bottom. Include small details such as a drone, airplane, rooftop solar panels, transit stations, pedestrians, and service vehicles. Use thin colored network lines running through the city to imply water, energy, data, logistics, and transit flows. Add exactly 5 visible circular numbered callout markers in the central scene: 6 on a building, 3 near a road/transport corridor, 8 near the right street edge, 9 in a green plaza, and 10 near an underground tunnel/canal edge.

Side information cards: Place exactly 6 visible rounded rectangular system cards around the city, each with a colored numbered badge, bilingual title, small bullet labels, and mini diagrams. Left column has 3 cards: 1 “能源系统 / Energy Grid” with solar panels, battery storage, EV charging, and a car; 2 “城市水循环 / Water Cycle” with water plant, rainwater collection, sewage treatment, reclaimed water, and groundwater diagrams; 3 “交通网络 / Transportation” with a subway train, bus, roads, bicycle icon, and route lines. Right column has 3 cards: 6 “建筑内部系统 / Building Systems” with a cutaway building showing floors, HVAC, fire safety, BMS control, and pipes; 7 “公共服务 / Public Services” with hospital, school, fire station, police station, and community service icons; 8 “食物与物流 / Food & Logistics” with warehouse, cold chain, last-mile delivery, truck, boxes, and robot delivery. Keep the lower portions of the left and right columns slightly cropped by the screenshot edge, matching a captured page view.

Visual style: Highly detailed semi-realistic isometric illustration mixed with technical infographic design, muted grays and whites with navy typography, accent colors yellow, cyan, red, purple, orange, and green. Text should be mostly legible at headline level but many small labels can be tiny. Maintain the feeling of an authentic Chinese web article showcasing an example image.

Constraints: Do not add browser address bars, watermarks, floating toolbars, or extra social-media UI. Preserve the Chinese headings on the webpage and the bilingual poster title hierarchy. The image should look like a screenshot of a generated infographic embedded in a documentation page.
```

### No. 43: Infographic / Edu Visual - Educational Lightning Storyboard Infographic

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A detailed storyboard prompt for creating professional educational infographics about lightning, featuring multiple scenes with visual descriptions, camera angles, and voiceover instructions.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a vertical 2:3 educational storyboard infographic, 5 horizontal scenes, cinematic realistic 3D science visualization, exactly like a professional educational video storyboard.

TOPIC: HOW LIGHTNING HAPPENS
Duration: 15 seconds

1 | 0:00–0:03 — STORM CLOUDS
Visual: Huge dark storm clouds forming above a landscape.
Action: Clouds grow rapidly with electric particles inside.
VO: “Lightning begins inside powerful storm clouds.”
SFX: Wind and distant thunder.
Camera: Slow aerial push in.
Transition: Light Flash.

2 | 0:03–0:06 — ELECTRIC CHARGE
Visual: Close-up inside cloud showing positive and negative charges separating.
Action: Blue and orange particles move to opposite sides.
Labels: POSITIVE CHARGE, NEGATIVE CHARGE.
VO: “Ice and water particles separate electric charges inside the cloud.”
SFX: Electric crackle.
Camera: Macro tracking.
Transition: Energy Glow.

3 | 0:06–0:09 — CHARGE BUILDS
Visual: Dark cloud above the ground, bright electrical energy increasing.
Action: Electric particles move downward toward Earth.
VO: “As the charge becomes stronger, electricity searches for a path.”
SFX: Rising electrical hum.
Camera: Tilt from cloud toward ground.
Transition: Quick Flash.

4 | 0:09–0:12 — LIGHTNING STRIKE
Visual: Bright lightning bolt connects cloud to ground.
Action: Lightning travels rapidly downward.
Label: LIGHTNING.
VO: “A powerful electrical discharge creates a lightning bolt.”
SFX: Loud thunder crack.
Camera: Fast Pull Back.
Transition: White Flash.

5 | 0:12–0:15 — THUNDERSTORM
Visual: Wide storm landscape with lightning and rain.
Action: Lightning flashes while rain falls below.
VO: “The sudden heating of air also creates the sound of thunder.”
SFX: Deep thunder rolling.
Camera: Wide aerial pull back.
Transition: Smooth Fade.

Bottom: NARRATOR (VO): Clear, calm, educational male voice.
```

### No. 51: Infographic / Edu Visual - Watercolor Isometric Deconstruction Poster

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A sophisticated prompt that deconstructs an uploaded image into an isometric watercolor study, styled like an academic or editorial research poster.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Process similarly:

Please transform each photo I upload into an independent high-end design poster, outputting each photo individually without multi-image stitching. The overall composition should use a 3:4 vertical layout, with the upper and lower areas split strictly 1:1, each occupying 50% of the frame.

The upper part retains the original photo, maintaining the subject's identity, structure, proportions, perspective, orientation, realism, natural lighting, and original color atmosphere, with only slight high-end photographic color grading to give it the texture of an art magazine, independent publication, or exhibition photography. To fit the frame, the sky, ground, or environmental background can be naturally extended, but the subject must not be stretched, distorted, or changed, and no other objects should be mixed in or original content replaced.

The lower part extracts the most recognizable subject, outline, pose, and narrative relationship from the photo, reconstructing it as a structural deconstruction watercolor study following isometric logic. The uploaded original photo is the sole basis; the subject's identity, geometric form, proportions, observation direction, spatial relationship, primary colors, and scale references must come only from the original image. Do not paste the photo directly, and do not create before-and-after comparisons, collages, or photographic slices.

When redrawing, first identify the core subject, main axes, original observation direction, foreground/midground/background relationships, 3–7 most important identifying features, 4–6 primary colors, and environmental cues that reflect scale. Retain the subject's overall silhouette, general proportions, original perspective, symmetrical or asymmetrical relationships, key component relationships, identity-defining details, and primary material colors, so that it is instantly recognizable as the same object from the upper half.

The subject adopts isometric redrawing logic, reorganized with clear, restrained, and professional axonometric spatial expression, while remaining faithful to the original photo's structural relationships and viewing logic. Based on the subject's actual structure, deconstruct it into 2–5 meaningful complete levels or components: buildings, vessels, or combined objects can be deconstructed along roofs, floors, shells, frames, bases, modules, or connectors; landscapes or spaces can be unfolded by foreground, midground, background, and spatial enclosure layers; humans, animals, or plants should remain physically whole, expressed only through overlapping outlines, pose layers, action lines, and surface color blocks—dismemberment, dissection, or mechanical partitioning is prohibited. All layers should be separated with restraint along real axes or perspective directions, with uniform and moderate spacing, sharing the same central axis and spatial grid, and retaining one complete, stable, and most detailed base or anchor area so the audience can reassemble the subject in their mind.

The lower part's composition remains professional, restrained, and editorial. The subject is centered or slightly offset according to the original photo's center of gravity, with ample white space around it. The stable base is located at the bottom or center, and the separated structural layers expand upward, outward, or toward the depth along real axes. Avoid dramatic fragmentation, excessive exploded views, meaningless decoration, and mechanical disassembly. The overall look should be a highly finished research-oriented editorial poster rather than an engineering manual.

The painting language adopts the character of an architectural structural deconstruction watercolor study on a warm ivory watercolor paper background, combining clear line work, semi-transparent watercolor blocks, a few structural auxiliary lines, and restrained analytical markings. Retain slight paper grain, detailed brushstrokes, transparent layered colors, and rational white space to give it both the intellectual feel of a research diagram and the elegance of an art publication. Colors are extracted from the original photo and appropriately purified, remaining clear, accurate, restrained, and spirited, avoiding gray, muddy, heavy, over-rendered, or cheap CG effects.

Text naturally intervenes as part of the composition in the manner of research diagrams/editorial annotations. Extract a short title from the subject's identity, theme, action, state, or symbolic meaning from the photo, and pair it with a minimal amount of numbering, structural markers, lateral annotations, material words, state words, or miniature descriptions. The text remains slender, restrained, and professional, arranged horizontally, vertically, or in offset patterns along axes, layering directions, white space edges, or structural annotation positions, making it look like a blend of architectural research, watercolor observation, and high-end editorial design rather than a standard poster title.

The overall presentation features isometric structural logic, professional deconstructive order, watercolor study texture, editorial white space, and a high-end publishing vibe. Whether the subject is architecture, humans, animals, plants, objects, vehicles, street scenes, interiors, or natural landscapes, the lower half should grow the most appropriate deconstruction method from the original photo's own structure, maintaining high adaptability and portability. Avoid templated exploded views, complex tech UIs, feelings of dismemberment, mechanical parts, cartoonishness, e-commerce vibes, and unfounded structural fabrications.

Maintain a balance between the real subject, core meaning, micro-narrative, naive lines, and a large amount of white space. Regardless of whether the original image is a person, animal, plant, food, building, object, vehicle, or natural scene, first grasp "what this photo is truly about" before deciding how to reconstruct it, avoiding just capturing the object without the relationship, doing cute interactions without a theme, cartoon sticker vibes, complex decoration, and a templated feel.
```

### No. 77: Comic / Storyboard - Thematic Artistic Collage Template

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

An extensive template for creating artistic multi-scene collages based on a specific theme, allowing for varied layouts and visual scale while maintaining harmony.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
THEME = {argument name="theme" default="enter theme"}
STYLE = {argument name="style" default="enter the style you want"}
PALETTE = {argument name="palette" default="enter the color palette"}
ASPECT RATIO = {argument name="aspect ratio" default="enter the aspect ratio you want"}

DO:
  Create an artistic multi-scene collage built entirely around THEME.
  Derive subjects, environments, objects, characters and visual events naturally from THEME.
  Derive the emotional tone, atmosphere and energy naturally from THEME.
  Use people, animals, objects, landscapes, architecture, abstract forms or any combination of them when they meaningfully support THEME.
  Treat each scene as a distinct emotional or conceptual fragment from the same world.
  Build the composition like an expressive editorial art spread rather than a storyboard or regular panel grid.
  Freely vary the number, scale, proportions and placement of scenes.
  Combine large dominant images with narrow vertical fragments, wide horizontal crops, small detail scenes and partially cropped compositions.
  Stagger scene edges and shift their alignments to create an irregular visual rhythm.
  Let some scenes extend fully to the canvas edge while others terminate against neighboring imagery.
  Use occasional L-shaped, stepped or offset arrangements created naturally by neighboring scene proportions.
  Avoid repeating the same rectangle size or alignment across the composition.
  Keep scenes visually separate without visible frames or empty gaps between them.
  Let neighboring scenes meet through irregular crop relationships while keeping their environments independent.
  Do not blend separate locations into one continuous environment.
  Keep the main subjects of each scene visually contained within that scene.
  Use architecture, large color fields and negative space to shape the overall composition.
  Connect neighboring scenes through color relationships, visual rhythm and thematic echoes rather than physically merging their content.
  Choose environments and situations specifically suited to THEME.
  Include occasional intimate fragments such as hands, objects, windows, silhouettes, architectural details or empty spaces when they strengthen THEME.
  Give each scene a different visual scale, point of view and conceptual focus.
  Use expressive lighting, shape, perspective and negative space according to STYLE.
  Vary the emphasis of PALETTE from scene to scene while preserving overall harmony.
  Maintain consistency whenever subjects, characters, objects or locations recur.
  Keep the final composition visually clean, sophisticated and intentionally irregular.

CAMERA: varied editorial framing, environmental wides, cropped medium views, isolated details, unusual negative-space compositions, overhead fragments and off-center perspectives chosen according to THEME

RULES:
  Use STYLE as the authority for medium, linework, text
```

Panel 2: a darker dramatic cliff grotto scene with exactly 1 solitary black-haired woman reclining on white cloth at lower left, leaning forward with folded arms, beside a huge dark blue-black rock wall and a cascading vertical sheet of white fabric or surf falling into bright foamy water on the right. Panel 3: a coastal rock scene with exactly 1 black-haired woman in a vivid red draped dress reclining on blue-gray rocks, resting her head in one hand, her extremely long hair streaming far to the right in the wind; the background shows a calm blue sea with exactly 6 small white sailboats on the horizon and soft pale clouds.

```
Subject details: The women should look like serene mythological muses or sirens with porcelain skin, delicate faces, downcast or contemplative expressions, and graceful classical poses. ```
```
```
Create a flagship 2x2 campaign poster series for an original Vietnamese noodle brand named "PHO REPUBLIC", merging Orbit energy with Transit restraint. Use ultra-realistic commercial food photography, not illustration, not 3D render. The final artwork must present four coordinated square posters arranged in a strict 2x2 grid, each panel built from the same structural system with disciplined variation. The pho bowl must remain the absolute visual hero in every panel. Style lock: high-end graphic food advertising, bold editorial poster layout, product-first hierarchy, clean color-block backgrounds, oversized kinetic typography, youthful premium Asian street-dining branding, Cannes-level print campaign execution. Core campaign structure: four square panels with alternating deep navy blue and warm mustard yellow backgrounds. Each panel contains one large white pho bowl shown from the exact same slightly top-down camera angle. Bowl size, bowl height, bowl perspective, rim thickness, bowl diameter, and visual weight must be perfectly consistent across all four posters. The container must sit at an equal visual scale in every panel, acting as the fixed campaign anchor. Variation is allowed only in typography rhythm, circular text paths, callout placement, and directional movement around the bowl. Transit logic: simplify every panel so the layout feels flagship and globally polished. Reduce small labels, reduce decorative callouts, shorten copy, preserve more clean negative space, and let the bowl, background block, and primary typography do most of the work. Avoid flyer-like density. Each square should feel graphic, premium, and instantly readable. Orbit logic: create a coordinated movement system across the full 2x2 set. Panel one uses a strong descending diagonal text flow, panel two uses a mirrored inward-driving diagonal, panel three uses a controlled circular orbit around the bowl, and panel four uses a tighter circular return path that visually resolves the campaign. The four panels should feel like one connected visual sequence: launch, counter, orbit, resolve. Motion must feel crisp and graphic, not noisy. Food design: create a premium beef pho hero dish with richly detailed ingredients: glowing amber broth, thin rice noodles, sliced beef, beef balls, tendon or tripe accents, basil leaves, scallions, red chili slices, onion, fresh herbs, and subtle broth sheen. Ingredient styling may shift slightly between panels, but the bowl size and bowl dominance must never change. The dish must feel abundant, fragrant, hot, and highly craveable. Panel logic: 1. Top-left panel: deep navy background, oversized stacked headline moving diagonally from upper-left toward center, bowl anchored in the lower-middle zone, a few restrained callout markers near the rim. 2. Top-right panel: mustard background, mirrored bold typography driving inward from upper-right toward center seam, bowl anchored at the same scale and height as panel one, minimal graphic accents only. 3. Bottom-left panel: mustard background, centered bowl with a clean circular text orbit around it, fewer callouts than the top row, stronger negative space. 4. Bottom-right panel: deep navy background, centered bowl matching panel three exactly in scale and placement, tighter orbit text and a compact brand footer block to conclude the campaign. Typography and branding: use original English text only, never copy any existing wording. Use a bold master phrase such as "TALK THE PHO" as the campaign anchor across the series, varied in scale and rhythm but always consistent in brand voice. Add only a few short descriptors such as "fresh", "slow broth", "street soul", "herbal", and "comfort". Include a compact brand block reading "PHO REPUBLIC" with a subtitle such as "modern Vietnamese kitchen". Typography should feel oversized, architectural, witty, and kinetic, but always secondary to the bowls. Lighting: bright controlled studio food lighting with soft frontal illumination and subtle side shaping. The broth must glow warmly, herbs must feel crisp and fresh, meat textures must remain moist and realistic, and the white ceramic bowls must look clean, glossy, and dimensional. Keep shadows soft and controlled, with no muddy contrast. Materials and realism: realistic white ceramic bowls with identical shape and scale across all four posters, hot glossy broth, tender beef textures, springy noodles, fresh basil, crisp chili slices, subtle oil shimmer, clean matte background fields, premium print-ready sharpness, and integrated graphic elements that feel designed rather than pasted. Color system: strict alternating navy blue and mustard yellow fields, with restrained accents of red, white, black, and herb green. Keep the palette disciplined, high-contrast, and premium. Food colors must remain natural and appetizing. Quality and physical rules: all four panels must feel like one unified flagship campaign. Equal bowl size across every panel is mandatory. Equal bowl camera angle, equal bowl scale, equal bowl visual weight, and equal product dominance must be preserved. Keep the campaign clean, graphic, premium, and highly legible. No clutter, no warped rims, no type collisions, no weak food detail, no dead black patches, no oversaturated broth. Negative prompt: copied branding, copied text, inconsistent bowl sizes, inconsistent bowl height, inconsistent bowl perspective, different container scale between panels, warped bowl rims, blurry food, fake broth texture, messy layout, chaotic typography, cheap flyer look, low-resolution print feel, oversaturated food colors, excessive labels, cluttered callouts, cartoon rendering, CGI look, illustration style, product losing dominance, muddy shadows, dirty background.
```

```
PROTECTED WARDROBE RULE — HIGHEST PRIORITY: Treat each garment description as immutable source identity. Preserve construction, parts, hardware, material, pattern, and every registered graphic or marking—its surface, side, orientation, scale, colors, content, spelling, and count—unless a later instruction explicitly changes color, material, or wearing state. A MATERIAL CHANGE substitutes only the explicitly scoped source material phrase and preserves every other garment attribute. FINAL WORN STATE overrides only the scoped item's use, position, side, orientation, fastening, layering, folds, tucks, knots, and drape. A SOURCE GARMENT label describes the unstyled item, never the completed silhouette. Do not redesign, add, remove, mirror, duplicate, relocate, or redraw protected details.

Wardrobe:

Outerwear / layers:

One oversized upper-thigh-length technical bomber jacket in high-gloss hot fuchsia, glossy neutral black, vivid teal, silver gray, muted yellow, and small white accents. ```
```
```
Create a flagship commercial advertising poster for an original ready-to-drink coffee brand named "RISE SHOT". Use ultra-realistic commercial lifestyle photography, not illustration, not 3D render. Merge Transit restraint with Orbit energy while converting all headline and campaign text into English only. Keep the same core structural logic: a bright modern office meeting room with softly blurred charts and presentation boards in the background; one elegant young professional woman standing slightly right of center, smiling with calm confidence while holding a slim coffee can near her face; in front of her, a monochrome grayscale tired office-version figure sits behind an open laptop, representing pre-coffee fatigue; three color-variant coffee cans are aligned in the lower-left foreground; a bold oversized English headline occupies the upper-left to upper-center area; a red angled English campaign tag sits near the lower center. The final image must feel like a Cannes-level FMCG office-energy campaign: witty, aspirational, clean, premium, and product-led. Style lock: ultra-realistic commercial product-and-portrait photography, premium office lifestyle advertising, bright soft daylight interior, clean corporate aesthetic, sharp packaging realism, strong typography hierarchy, contemporary global campaign design. Core composition: vertical poster layout, one standing woman in the mid-right hero zone, one seated grayscale alter-ego figure centered lower-middle behind a laptop, one hero can in the woman’s hand near shoulder height, three SKU cans aligned in the lower-left foreground, large English headline at the top, minimal brand mark in the upper-right, one red callout strip near the lower center. The in-hand coffee can and the bottom SKU lineup must remain the true product heroes, while the woman acts as the aspirational energy bridge. Transit logic: simplify the desk surface, reduce background chart detail, keep the room bright and quiet, shorten all copy, and let the can, the woman, and the before-after contrast carry the campaign. Avoid a cluttered office-ad feeling. Use negative space, clean hierarchy, and disciplined layout to create flagship impact. Orbit logic: build a subtle but clear visual flow from the top headline into the standing woman’s face, then to the can in her hand, down through the grayscale seated figure, and finally into the three cans at bottom left. Add only a very restrained transition effect between the grayscale and color states, such as faint light fragments or minimal tonal drift, to imply motion and transformation without visual noise. Concept logic: show the contrast between "low-energy work mode" and "switched-on performance mode" within one premium frame. The grayscale seated figure feels drained, mentally flat, and stuck at work, while the standing color figure feels refreshed, sharp, productive, and effortlessly confident after coffee. The transition should feel clean, elegant, and believable. Female character: one realistic young adult office woman with natural anatomy, correct shoulders, neck, arms, and hand proportions, natural five fingers, clear finger joints, realistic skin texture, subtle pores, natural lip texture, soft under-eye detail, and believable hair strands. She wears a light tailored blazer over a minimal office top. Expression: relaxed, knowing smile, bright but not exaggerated. Pose: upright, elegant, one arm bent naturally holding the can, body slightly angled, gaze subtly upward-left with confident satisfaction. Alter-ego seated figure: one realistic grayscale office-worn female figure seated behind a laptop at the desk, leaning on one hand with visible fatigue and boredom. Keep her desaturated, softly sculpted, and cleanly integrated into the scene. She should feel like the same person in an earlier drained state, not a separate character. Lighting: soft premium office daylight from upper-left front with gentle ambient fill across the room. The standing woman and coffee can receive the clearest and brightest light. The seated grayscale figure is slightly flatter and dimmer but still readable. Use delicate highlight control on the aluminum cans, hair, blazer folds, laptop edge, and desk surface. No harsh shadows, no muddy haze. Materials and realism: highly realistic slim aluminum coffee cans with commissioned printed labels, subtle metallic reflections, visible rim highlights, and a premium satin finish. Clean matte desk surface with only minimal papers and office items. Soft-focus charts and graphs behind. Clothing shows natural fabric folds and light texture. Skin remains natural, de-AI, and photographic. Color strategy: warm ivory and soft beige office background, cool pale-gray blazer tones, rich can accent colors such as amber gold, forest green, and deep red, plus a strong red campaign tag. The grayscale seated figure provides the main contrast device. Keep the palette disciplined, modern, and premium. Typography and layout: use original English text only, never copy any existing wording. Add a bold top headline such as “ONE SIP. FULL IMPACT.” with a small supporting line like “Switch on. Stay sharp.” Place a red angled tag near the lower center with short copy such as "QUICK BOOST / READY NOW". Add a minimal brand logo in the upper-right. Typography must feel bold, global, and structured, but always secondary to the coffee can and character action. Atmosphere: sharp, witty, modern office confidence, productivity boost, premium convenience coffee, polished workplace aspiration, clean and instantly readable. Quality and physical rules: extremely sharp hero can, readable labels, realistic metallic material, correct hand anatomy, five fingers only, natural face proportions, clean separation between standing figure and grayscale seated figure, believable perspective, no clutter, no cheap flyer feel, no muddy whites, no dead black patches, no distorted laptop, no warped cans, no broken text, no extra limbs. The coffee product must remain visually dominant. Negative prompt: copied brand name, copied slogan, copied packaging text, celebrity face, deformed hands, extra fingers, fused fingers, warped can shape, unreadable label, plastic skin, bad anatomy, messy office, cluttered desk, muddy lighting, low-resolution packaging, cartoon rendering, CGI look, illustration style, chaotic composition, weak product hierarchy, grayscale figure overpowering product, random props, dirty background.
```

### No. 101: Product Marketing - 3D Paper and Clay Plaque

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A specialized prompt for creating premium, handcrafted 3D paper and soft clay destination plaques with consistent typography and geometric framing.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a premium, cute, handcrafted 3D paper-and-soft-clay destination plaque for {argument name="location" default="[LOCATION]"}, designed as part of one consistent global collectible series.

COMPOSITION & GEOMETRY: Use a fixed horizontal framed layout on a normalized 100%100 grid. Frame: x=5–95, y=8–92. Landmark zone: y=18–58. Foreground: y=48–67. Location-name zone: y=68–88. Bottom margin: y=88–92. Keep these proportions identical for every location.

LANDMARK HIERARCHY: Place 1 centered hero landmark at x=50, occupying 34–38% height; 2 supporting landmarks at x=25 and x=75, each 22–28% height; 2–4 smaller background elements across x=10–90, each 12–20% height; and 1–3 subtle local foreground details, no more than 14% height. Use only the most recognizable and geographically appropriate features of {argument name="location" default="[LOCATION]"}. Preserve defining silhouettes while simplifying details and avoiding clutter, clichés, or stereotypes.

TYPOGRAPHY: Display exactly “{argument name="location" default="[LOCATION]"}”, centered and unobstructed. The lettering should span approximately 82–86% of the plaque width and 17–20% of the canvas height. Use large, raised, embossed, rounded sans-serif lettering in warm ivory with a matte finish. Maintain consistent weight, spacing, alignment, and baseline across the entire series.

MATERIAL & STYLE: Approximately 70% layered paper and 30% soft matte clay. Create a shallow-relief handcrafted appearance with visible paper fibers, subtle clay texture, rounded handmade edges, delicate layer shadows, and small natural imperfections. Avoid glossy plastic, metallic materials, photorealism, or a generic CGI appearance.

COLOR PALETTE: Warm ivory/cream foundation, soft beige and natural paper tones, muted blue-gray secondary accents, plus minimal muted colors inspired by the specific location.

CAMERA & LIGHTING: Straight-on centered camera with fixed perspective and scale. Use a warm neutral background, soft diffused studio lighting, gentle shadows, consistent depth, and subtle dimensional separation. No reflections, dramatic perspective, or surrounding environment.

SERIES CONSISTENCY: Keep the frame, composition, grid, typography, landmark scale, hierarchy, object density, materials, palette, lighting, camera angle, depth, shadows, and negative space identical across every location. Change only the landmarks, culturally/environmentally relevant details, and restrained location-specific accent colors.

FINAL QUALITY CHECK: {argument name="location" default="[LOCATION]"} must be instantly recognizable, correctly spelled, visually balanced, and free of unnecessary text. Maintain the fixed landmark hierarchy, unobstructed lettering, consistent proportions, premium handcrafted texture, cute collectible aesthetic, and cohesive global-series identity.
```

```
Wardrobe:

Wardrobe assembly rule:
Each FINAL WORN STATE overrides conflicting wearing-state wording only for the item descriptions it explicitly scopes or names, and only for wearing role or use, count, position, side, orientation, routing, fastening, layering, folds, tucking, and draping. Preserve those descriptions for identity, design, material, color, pattern, construction, and proportions.

Top:

One solid white cropped bustier top constructed from fine translucent floral lace and narrow opaque woven binding. The silhouette is close and compact, with a shallow rectangular front envelope, two broad straight straps, and a continuous narrow lower band. A low softly squared neckline spans between the straps. The front is divided into two shallow curved lace panels by a narrow center bridge, with a horizontal opaque binding defining the lower edge of the panels and a second binding completing the base. Dense floral-and-leaf lace fills every framed panel, retaining small irregular transparent cells between the embroidered threads. The broad straps continue into the rear band, where two narrow white tie extensions form one compact bow with two balanced loops and two short finished tails. Keep the lace panels flat and distinct, the binding widths consistent, the strap paths independent, the rear bow compact, and the open lacework crisp rather than blurred into solid white fabric.

One pale sky-blue and white pinstripe longline button-front shirt in fine, lightly crinkled cotton shirting, with dense narrow white stripes running longitudinally across the main panels and sleeves and following the cut direction of the collar, placket, and cuffs. The cut is relaxed and spacious, with dropped shoulders, a straight upper section, and softly expanding volume through the lower section. A broad pointed collar frames a short exposed upper placket that continues into a full center-front opening. Long sleeves follow a generous straight line from low armscyes and narrow into deep rectangular cuffs. Clean side seams, a narrow stitched front placket, and an extended shirttail hem with curved front and rear edges and short side separations complete the panels. Small matte cream four-hole buttons are evenly spaced along the placket. Preserve the fine stripe spacing, crisp pattern alignment, airy shirt volume, clean collar points, controlled cuff structure, and dry woven surface without swollen seams, melted stripes, or fuzzy edges.

Final worn state:
For the second item description above only:
- Front closure: Engage every existing front closure from the top edge to the bottom edge and align the two front edges continuously on the anatomical center front. Leave no open interval, offset panel, or invented fastening. FINAL FASTENING STATE: every selected closed position is physically connected to its matching receiver; every selected open position is visibly disconnected, and panel overlap does not imitate a fastening.
- Sleeves: Release both cuff and sleeve-placket closures, then turn each sleeve outward in broad folds. Position both rolled bands immediately below the elbow points. Preserve both cuffs and keep every fold below the elbows. Do not push either roll above an elbow, hide a cuff, or leave one sleeve at the wrist.
- Layering: Layer this garment underneath the first item description under Top across the upper body regardless of whether either garment has a front opening. Preserve each garment's selected or source-default closure state and expose only the inner garment's neckline, collar, cuffs, sleeves, or hem where the outer garment does not physically cover them. Change only the inside-outside order; do not invent an opening or fastening, merge the garments, or route either garment through the other's neckline, armhole, sleeve, seam, or panel.
- Finish — Natural: Use a few broad soft folds with modest overlap and small large-scale asymmetry while preserving every cuff, hem, seam, and hardware component.

For the second item description under Top and the first item description under Top together only:
- Finish — Relaxed: At the real upper-body overlap at the neckline, armholes, sides, and hems, compress mainly this covered inner garment and let the outer target yield only locally; let only a few broad folds leave those boundaries and descend within each garment's own panels. Preserve both garments' necklines, armholes, straps, sleeves, seams, closures, and hems. Use light stable contact and broader material-correct ease without changing the selected layer order.

Bottom: One charcoal-grey two-tier pleated mini skirt made from lightweight opaque woven fabric with a smooth matte-to-soft-luster face, crisp fold memory, and fluid movement. A clean fitted waistband supports a full upper tier formed from broad regular knife pleats that open into a short circular flare. A second pleated tier is joined beneath the first tier and expands to a slightly deeper lower flare, producing two clearly separated horizontal levels without merging their pleat systems. Every tier edge is finished with one narrow white gathered ruffle tape that remains continuously visible as a bright border against the grey ground. One small muted lavender-grey rectangular woven label is centered on a lower-front outer section, framed by a narrow white scalloped border and carrying the exact two-line inscription “Moonlit” in large white cursive above “Daydream” in smaller white cursive. Keep both tiers evenly distributed, the pleat ridges sharply legible, the white edge trims narrow and continuous, and the rear construction consistent with the same two-tier pleated assembly.

Legwear: One matching pair of warm-ivory knee-high socks, displayed side by side as complete footed products. Opaque fine-rib knit with a soft matte finish, straight tubular shafts, gently shaped heels and toes, and wide plain elasticized cuffs with a subtle folded appearance. Uniform warm-ivory color throughout, tonal linking at the toes, no logos, no stripes, no lace, no graphics.

Footwear: Create one anatomically mirrored matching pair of white low-top lace-up sneakers with rounded shell toes and flat white rubber cupsoles. Build each upper from smooth white leather panels with a low padded collar, seven paired lace positions, flat white laces, a straight central tongue, and a ribbed molded rubber shell covering the complete toe cap. Apply three parallel black stepped bands to the outer quarter, each band formed from two solid diagonal rectangular segments separated by one narrow vertical gap; keep the three bands equal in width and evenly spaced. Add one compact white rectangular tongue label printed with the clearly legible uppercase word “SOLSTICE” in black condensed lettering. Finish the sole with a fine textured sidewall and shallow dark outsole edge, keeping the complete pair identical except for anatomical mirroring, with precise panel stitching, balanced lacing, clean black-white separation, and no graphics outside the confirmed quarter and tongue zones.

Bag:

One compact metallic-silver mini tote bag with a softly structured rectangular body, narrow side gussets, rounded lower corners, and a flat reinforced base. Smooth reflective synthetic leather covers the body with crisp silver edge binding and tonal stitching. Two short rolled top handles attach through rectangular stitched tabs, while one long narrow adjustable strap connects to small silver swivel clasps at the upper side rings and includes a matching slider buckle. A single silver zipper closes the straight top opening and terminates at compact end tabs. Keep the broad faces plain and uninterrupted, with pale-gray lining and no text, patches, appliqués, or decorative ornaments.

Final worn state:
- Bag carry: VIEW: FRONT/NEAR-FRONT SUPPO
```

Necklace: A delicate short silver-tone jewelry chain formed as one continuous fine strand with closely spaced micro-links and intermittent tiny elongated bead stations. Keep the chain light, even, and bilaterally balanced from a subtle central station, with no pendant or oversized focal component. Complete it with a compact lobster clasp and a short matching adjustment tail. Maintain uniform link pitch, smooth articulation, secure bead-to-chain joins, restrained polished reflections, and a refined low-profile finish. One medium-length antique-silver pendant necklace constructed as a complete flexible closed circuit. A fine oxidized cable chain with compact oval links closes through a small lobster clasp and short extender. Center one sculptural eye-shaped pendant composed of a narrow pointed oval outer frame, a raised polished upper lid, a dark recessed almond-shaped center, and a tiny silver bead pupil, connected through a compact integrated bail. Preserve the horizontal pendant orientation, dark-and-bright metal contrast, delicate chain scale, and absence of gemstones, lettering, or additional charms. Earrings: One matching pair of large polished warm-gold hoop earrings, shown together as two complete identical products. Each earring forms a slender circular open hoop from uniform round-section metal, with one straight post at the upper terminal and a compact hinged latch at the opposing terminal. Preserve the broad clean circle, lightweight section, mirror-polished warm-gold finish, identical diameter, and absence of gemstones, texture, lettering, or dangling components.

### No. 120: E-commerce Main Image - Realistic Unaju Grilled Eel Box

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A photorealistic restaurant-style food shot of glossy grilled eel over rice in a lacquered bento box for menu or promotional use.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a realistic high-resolution food photography image of {argument name="dish name" default="unaju, Japanese grilled eel over rice"} served in a rectangular black lacquered bento box with a thin red inner rim, placed on a warm dark wooden restaurant table. The box is filled with white rice and topped edge-to-edge with exactly 4 long grilled eel fillets arranged horizontally in parallel, slightly overlapping, coated in a glossy caramel-brown tare sauce with visible char marks, tiny blistered skin texture, subtle grill lines, and faint skewer marks. The eel should look moist, oily, and sizzling, with bright specular highlights and appetizing lacquered reflections. Use a close-up three-quarter overhead angle, shallow depth of field, natural warm restaurant lighting, and rich contrast. In the softly blurred background include exactly 3 supporting table items: a small white side dish at the upper left with pale pickles or garnish, a dark cylindrical cup near the upper center, and a black soup bowl at the upper right containing clear broth and a small green herb leaf. Keep the focus sharply on the eel and lacquer box, with no people, no utensils, no text, and no watermark.
```
